根据报错信息修正代码,仅给出需要修正的地方;

## 回答
根据报错信息，以下是需要修正的地方：

1. **`quaternion_to_angle_axis` 和 `angle_axis_to_quaternion` 函数的参数类型不匹配**：
   - 这些函数期望的参数是固定长度的数组 `&[T; 4]` 和 `&mut [T; 3]`，但传入的是切片 `&[f64]` 和 `&mut [f64]`。
   - 需要将切片转换为固定长度的数组。

   ```rust
   // 修正 quaternion_to_angle_axis 的调用
   let quaternion: [f64; 4] = [
       self.parameters[10 * i as usize],
       self.parameters[10 * i as usize + 1],
       self.parameters[10 * i as usize + 2],
       self.parameters[10 * i as usize + 3],
   ];
   let mut angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [0.0; 3];
   quaternion_to_angle_axis(&quaternion, &mut angle_axis_arr);
   angleaxis[0..3].copy_from_slice(&angle_axis_arr);
   ```

   ```rust
   // 修正 angle_axis_to_quaternion 的调用
   let angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [angle_axis[0], angle_axis[1], angle_axis[2]];
   let mut quaternion_arr: [f64; 4] = [0.0; 4];
   angle_axis_to_quaternion(&angle_axis_arr, &mut quaternion_arr);
   camera[0..4].copy_from_slice(&quaternion_arr);
   ```

2. **`angle_axis_rotate_point` 函数的参数类型不匹配**：
   - 该函数期望的参数是固定长度的数组 `&[T; 3]`，但传入的是切片 `&[f64]` 和 `&mut [f64]`。
   - 需要将切片转换为固定长度的数组。

   ```rust
   // 修正 angle_axis_rotate_point 的调用
   let angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [angle_axis[0], angle_axis[1], angle_axis[2]];
   let pt_arr: [f64; 3] = [
       camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 6],
       camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 5],
       camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 4],
   ];
   let mut result_arr: [f64; 3] = [0.0; 3];
   angle_axis_rotate_point(&angle_axis_arr, &pt_arr, &mut result_arr);
   center.copy_from_slice(&result_arr);
   ```

3. **`norm_l1` 方法不存在**：
   - `norm_l1` 方法不存在，应该使用 `norm` 方法。

   ```rust
   // 修正 norm_l1 的调用
   tmp[i as usize] = (point - median_point).norm();
   ```

4. **借用冲突问题**：
   - 在 `perturb` 方法中，`self.num_points` 和 `self.camera_block_size()` 被同时借用为不可变和可变引用，导致冲突。
   - 需要在借用之前将 `self.num_points` 和 `self.camera_block_size()` 的值存储在局部变量中。

   ```rust
   // 修正 perturb 方法中的借用冲突
   let num_points = self.num_points;
   let camera_block_size = self.camera_block_size();
   let points = self.mutable_points();
   if point_sigma > 0.0 {
       for i in 0..num_points {
           perturb_point3(point_sigma, &mut points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3]);
       }
   }

   for i in 0..self.num_cameras {
       let camera = &mut self.parameters[camera_block_size as usize * i as usize..];
       let mut angle_axis = [0.0; 3];
       let mut center = [0.0; 3];
       self.camera_to_angle_axis_and_center(camera, &mut angle_axis, &mut center);

       if rotation_sigma > 0.0 {
           perturb_point3(rotation_sigma, &mut angle_axis);
       }
       self.angle_axis_and_center_to_camera(&angle_axis, &center, camera);

       if translation_sigma > 0.0 {
           perturb_point3(translation_sigma, &mut camera[camera_block_size as usize - 6..]);
       }
   }
   ```

5. **`mutable_camera_for_observation` 和 `mutable_point_for_observation` 方法中的借用冲突**：
   - 这些方法中，`self.camera_block_size()` 和 `self.point_block_size()` 被同时借用为不可变和可变引用，导致冲突。
   - 需要在借用之前将 `self.camera_block_size()` 和 `self.point_block_size()` 的值存储在局部变量中。

   ```rust
   // 修正 mutable_camera_for_observation 方法中的借用冲突
   pub fn mutable_camera_for_observation(&mut self, i: usize) -> &mut [f64] {
       let camera_block_size = self.camera_block_size();
       let start = self.camera_index[i] as usize * camera_block_size as usize;
       &mut self.parameters[start..start + camera_block_size as usize]
   }

   // 修正 mutable_point_for_observation 方法中的借用冲突
   pub fn mutable_point_for_observation(&mut self, i: usize) -> &mut [f64] {
       let camera_block_size = self.camera_block_size();
       let point_block_size = self.point_block_size();
       let start = camera_block_size as usize * self.num_cameras as usize + self.point_index[i] as usize * point_block_size as usize;
       &mut self.parameters[start..start + point_block_size as usize]
   }
   ```

通过这些修正，代码应该能够成功编译。


# 问题
```rs
/* start BAL问题 */

/// 从文件读入BAL dataset
pub struct BALProblem {
    num_cameras: i32,
    num_points: i32,
    num_observations: i32,
    num_parameters: i32,
    use_quaternions: bool,

    point_index: Vec<i32>,
    camera_index: Vec<i32>,
    observations: Vec<f64>,
    parameters: Vec<f64>,
}

impl BALProblem {
    /// 从文本文件加载BAL数据
    pub fn new(filename: &str, use_quaternions: bool) -> Self {
        // 打开文件
        let mut file = File::open(filename).expect("Unable to open file");
        let mut reader = BufReader::new(file);

        let mut num_cameras = 0;
        let mut num_points = 0;
        let mut num_observations = 0;

        // 读取头信息
        let mut line = String::new();
        reader.read_line(&mut line).expect("Unable to read header");
        let header: Vec<&str> = line.trim().split_whitespace().collect();
        num_cameras = header[0].parse().unwrap();
        num_points = header[1].parse().unwrap();
        num_observations = header[2].parse().unwrap();

        let mut point_index = vec![0; num_observations as usize];
        let mut camera_index = vec![0; num_observations as usize];
        let mut observations = vec![0.0; 2 * num_observations as usize];

        let num_parameters = 9 * num_cameras + 3 * num_points;
        let mut parameters = vec![0.0; num_parameters as usize];

        // 读取观测数据
        for i in 0..num_observations {
            let mut line = String::new();
            reader.read_line(&mut line).expect("Unable to read observation");
            let obs: Vec<&str> = line.trim().split_whitespace().collect();
            camera_index[i as usize] = obs[0].parse().unwrap();
            point_index[i as usize] = obs[1].parse().unwrap();
            observations[2 * i as usize] = obs[2].parse().unwrap();
            observations[2 * i as usize + 1] = obs[3].parse().unwrap();
        }

        // 读取参数数据
        for i in 0..num_parameters {
            let mut line = String::new();
            reader.read_line(&mut line).expect("Unable to read parameter");
            parameters[i as usize] = line.trim().parse().unwrap();
        }

        if use_quaternions {
            // 将角轴转换为四元数
            let num_parameters = 10 * num_cameras + 3 * num_points;
            let mut quaternion_parameters = vec![0.0; num_parameters as usize];
            let mut original_cursor = 0;
            let mut quaternion_cursor = 0;

            for _ in 0..num_cameras {

                let angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [parameters[original_cursor], parameters[original_cursor + 1], parameters[original_cursor + 2]];
                let mut quaternion_arr: [f64; 4] = [0.0; 4];
                angle_axis_to_quaternion(&angle_axis_arr, &mut quaternion_arr);

                quaternion_cursor += 4;
                original_cursor += 3;
                for j in 4..10 {
                    quaternion_parameters[quaternion_cursor] = parameters[original_cursor];
                    quaternion_cursor += 1;
                    original_cursor += 1;
                }
            }

            // 复制剩余的点数据
            for i in 0..3 * num_points {
                quaternion_parameters[quaternion_cursor] = parameters[original_cursor];
                quaternion_cursor += 1;
                original_cursor += 1;
            }

            parameters = quaternion_parameters;
        }

        BALProblem {
            num_cameras,
            num_points,
            num_observations,
            num_parameters,
            use_quaternions,
            point_index,
            camera_index,
            observations,
            parameters,
        }
    }

    /// 将结果保存到文本文件
    pub fn write_to_file(&self, filename: &str) -> io::Result<()> {
        let mut file = BufWriter::new(File::create(filename)?);

        writeln!(file, "{} {} {} {}", self.num_cameras, self.num_cameras, self.num_points, self.num_observations)?;

        for i in 0..self.num_observations {
            write!(file, "{} {}", self.camera_index[i as usize], self.point_index[i as usize])?;
            for j in 0..2 {
                write!(file, " {}", self.observations[2 * i as usize + j])?;
            }
            writeln!(file)?;
        }

        for i in 0..self.num_cameras() {
            let mut angleaxis = [0.0; 9];
            if self.use_quaternions {
                let quaternion: [f64; 4] = [
                    self.parameters[10 * i as usize],
                    self.parameters[10 * i as usize + 1],
                    self.parameters[10 * i as usize + 2],
                    self.parameters[10 * i as usize + 3],
                ];
                let mut angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [0.0; 3];
                quaternion_to_angle_axis(&quaternion, &mut angle_axis_arr);
                angleaxis[0..3].copy_from_slice(&angle_axis_arr);
                angleaxis[3..9].copy_from_slice(&self.parameters[10 * i as usize + 4..10 * i as usize + 10]);
            } else {
                angleaxis[0..9].copy_from_slice(&self.parameters[9 * i as usize..9 * i as usize + 9]);
            }
            for j in 0..9 {
                writeln!(file, "{:.16}", angleaxis[j])?;
            }
        }

        let points = &self.parameters[self.camera_block_size() as usize * self.num_cameras as usize..];
        for i in 0..self.num_points() {
            let point = &points[i as usize * self.point_block_size() as usize..];
            for j in 0..self.point_block_size() {
                writeln!(file, "{:.16}", point[j as usize])?;
            }
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    /// 将问题保存为PLY文件以便在Meshlab或CloudCompare中检查
    pub fn write_to_ply_file(&self, filename: &str) -> io::Result<()> {
        let mut file = BufWriter::new(File::create(filename)?);

        writeln!(file, "ply")?;
        writeln!(file, "format ascii 1.0")?;
        writeln!(file, "element vertex {}", self.num_cameras + self.num_points)?;
        writeln!(file, "property float x")?;
        writeln!(file, "property float y")?;
        writeln!(file, "property float z")?;
        writeln!(file, "property uchar red")?;
        writeln!(file, "property uchar green")?;
        writeln!(file, "property uchar blue")?;
        writeln!(file, "end_header")?;

        // 导出外参数据（即相机中心）为绿色点
        let mut angle_axis = [0.0; 3];
        let mut center = [0.0; 3];
        for i in 0..self.num_cameras() {
            let camera = &self.parameters[self.camera_block_size() as usize * i as usize..];
            self.camera_to_angle_axis_and_center(camera, &mut angle_axis, &mut center);
            writeln!(file, "{} {} {} 0 255 0", center[0], center[1], center[2])?;
        }

        // 导出结构（即3D点）为白色点
        let points = &self.parameters[self.camera_block_size() as usize * self.num_cameras as usize..];
        for i in 0..self.num_points() {
            let point = &points[i as usize * self.point_block_size() as usize..];
            for j in 0..self.point_block_size() {
                write!(file, "{} ", point[j as usize])?;
            }
            writeln!(file, "255 255 255")?;
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    /// 将相机参数转换为角轴和中心
    fn camera_to_angle_axis_and_center(&self, camera: &[f64], angle_axis: &mut [f64], center: &mut [f64]) {
        if self.use_quaternions {
            quaternion_to_angle_axis(camera, angle_axis);
        } else {
            angle_axis.copy_from_slice(&camera[0..3]);
        }

        // c = -R't
        let inverse_rotation = DVector::from_column_slice(angle_axis).map(|x| -x);
        // 修正 angle_axis_rotate_point 的调用
        let angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [angle_axis[0], angle_axis[1], angle_axis[2]];
        let pt_arr: [f64; 3] = [
            camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 6],
            camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 5],
            camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 4],
        ];
        let mut result_arr: [f64; 3] = [0.0; 3];
        angle_axis_rotate_point(&angle_axis_arr, &pt_arr, &mut result_arr);
        center.copy_from_slice(&result_arr);
    }

    /// 将角轴和中心转换为相机参数
    fn angle_axis_and_center_to_camera(&self, angle_axis: &[f64], center: &[f64], camera: &mut [f64]) {
        if self.use_quaternions {
            angle_axis_to_quaternion(angle_axis, &mut camera[0..4]);
        } else {
            camera[0..3].copy_from_slice(angle_axis);
        }

        // t = -R * c
        angle_axis_rotate_point(angle_axis, center, &mut camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 6..]);
        camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 6..].iter_mut().for_each(|x| *x *= -1.0);
    }

    /// 归一化
    pub fn normalize(&mut self) {
        // 计算几何的边际中值
        let mut tmp = vec![0.0; self.num_points as usize];
        let mut median_point = Vector3::zeros();
        let points = self.mutable_points();

        for i in 0..3 {
            for j in 0..self.num_points {
                tmp[j as usize] = points[3 * j as usize + i];
            }
            median_point[i] = median(&tmp);
        }

        for i in 0..self.num_points {
            let point = DVector::from_column_slice(&points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3]);
            tmp[i as usize] = (point - median_point).norm();
        }

        let median_absolute_deviation = median(&tmp);

        // 缩放使得结果的绝对中位差为100
        let scale = 100.0 / median_absolute_deviation;

        // X = scale * (X - median)
        for i in 0..self.num_points {
            let point = DVector::from_column_slice(&points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3]);
            let scaled_point = scale * (point - median_point);
            points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3].copy_from_slice(scaled_point.as_slice());
        }

        let cameras = self.mutable_cameras();
        let mut angle_axis = [0.0; 3];
        let mut center = [0.0; 3];
        for i in 0..self.num_cameras {
            let camera = &mut cameras[self.camera_block_size() as usize * i as usize..];
            self.camera_to_angle_axis_and_center(camera, &mut angle_axis, &mut center);
            // center = scale * (center - median)
            let scaled_center = scale * (DVector::from_column_slice(&center) - median_point);
            center.copy_from_slice(scaled_center.as_slice());
            self.angle_axis_and_center_to_camera(&angle_axis, &center, camera);
        }
    }

    /// 扰动
    pub fn perturb(&mut self, rotation_sigma: f64, translation_sigma: f64, point_sigma: f64) {
        assert!(point_sigma >= 0.0);
        assert!(rotation_sigma >= 0.0);
        assert!(translation_sigma >= 0.0);

        let points = self.mutable_points();
        if point_sigma > 0.0 {
            for i in 0..self.num_points {
                perturb_point3(point_sigma, &mut points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3]);
            }
        }

        for i in 0..self.num_cameras {
            let camera = &mut self.parameters[self.camera_block_size() as usize * i as usize..];

            let mut angle_axis = [0.0; 3];
            let mut center = [0.0; 3];
            self.camera_to_angle_axis_and_center(camera, &mut angle_axis, &mut center);

            if rotation_sigma > 0.0 {
                perturb_point3(rotation_sigma, &mut angle_axis);
            }
            self.angle_axis_and_center_to_camera(&angle_axis, &center, camera);

            if translation_sigma > 0.0 {
                perturb_point3(translation_sigma, &mut camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 6..]);
            }
        }
    }

    /// 获取相机块大小
    pub fn camera_block_size(&self) -> i32 {
        if self.use_quaternions { 10 } else { 9 }
    }

    /// 获取点块大小
    pub fn point_block_size(&self) -> i32 {
        3
    }

    /// 获取相机数量
    pub fn num_cameras(&self) -> i32 {
        self.num_cameras
    }

    /// 获取点数量
    pub fn num_points(&self) -> i32 {
        self.num_points
    }

    /// 获取观测数量
    pub fn num_observations(&self) -> i32 {
        self.num_observations
    }

    /// 获取参数数量
    pub fn num_parameters(&self) -> i32 {
        self.num_parameters
    }

    /// 获取点索引
    pub fn point_index(&self) -> &[i32] {
        &self.point_index
    }

    /// 获取相机索引
    pub fn camera_index(&self) -> &[i32] {
        &self.camera_index
    }

    /// 获取观测数据
    pub fn observations(&self) -> &[f64] {
        &self.observations
    }

    /// 获取参数数据
    pub fn parameters(&self) -> &[f64] {
        &self.parameters
    }

    /// 获取相机参数
    pub fn cameras(&self) -> &[f64] {
        &self.parameters
    }

    /// 获取点参数
    pub fn points(&self) -> &[f64] {
        &self.parameters[self.camera_block_size() as usize * self.num_cameras as usize..]
    }

    /// 获取可变的相机参数
    pub fn mutable_cameras(&mut self) -> &mut [f64] {
        &mut self.parameters
    }

    /// 获取可变的点参数
    pub fn mutable_points(&mut self) -> &mut [f64] {
        let start = self.camera_block_size() as usize * self.num_cameras as usize;
        &mut self.parameters[start..]
    }

    /// 获取可变的相机参数（针对特定观测）
    pub fn mutable_camera_for_observation(&mut self, i: usize) -> &mut [f64] {
        let start = self.camera_index[i] as usize * self.camera_block_size() as usize;
        &mut self.parameters[start..start + self.camera_block_size() as usize]
    }

    /// 获取可变的点参数（针对特定观测）
    pub fn mutable_point_for_observation(&mut self, i: usize) -> &mut [f64] {
        let start = self.camera_block_size() as usize * self.num_cameras as usize + self.point_index[i] as usize * self.point_block_size() as usize;
        &mut self.parameters[start..start + self.point_block_size() as usize]
    }

    /// 获取相机参数（针对特定观测）
    pub fn camera_for_observation(&self, i: usize) -> &[f64] {
        let start = self.camera_index[i] as usize * self.camera_block_size() as usize;
        &self.parameters[start..start + self.camera_block_size() as usize]
    }

    /// 获取点参数（针对特定观测）
    pub fn point_for_observation(&self, i: usize) -> &[f64] {
        let start = self.camera_block_size() as usize * self.num_cameras as usize + self.point_index[i] as usize * self.point_block_size() as usize;
        &self.parameters[start..start + self.point_block_size() as usize]
    }
}

/// 扰动3D点
fn perturb_point3(sigma: f64, point: &mut [f64]) {
    for i in 0..3 {
        point[i] += rand_normal() * sigma;
    }
}

/// 计算中位数
fn median(data: &[f64]) -> f64 {
    let mut data = data.to_vec();
    data.sort_by(|a, b| a.partial_cmp(b).unwrap());
    data[data.len() / 2]
}
```

# 问题
```rs
/* start BAL问题 */

/// 从文件读入BAL dataset
pub struct BALProblem {
    num_cameras: i32,
    num_points: i32,
    num_observations: i32,
    num_parameters: i32,
    use_quaternions: bool,

    point_index: Vec<i32>,
    camera_index: Vec<i32>,
    observations: Vec<f64>,
    parameters: Vec<f64>,
}

impl BALProblem {
    /// 从文本文件加载BAL数据
    pub fn new(filename: &str, use_quaternions: bool) -> Self {
        // 打开文件
        let mut file = File::open(filename).expect("Unable to open file");
        let mut reader = BufReader::new(file);

        let mut num_cameras = 0;
        let mut num_points = 0;
        let mut num_observations = 0;

        // 读取头信息
        let mut line = String::new();
        reader.read_line(&mut line).expect("Unable to read header");
        let header: Vec<&str> = line.trim().split_whitespace().collect();
        num_cameras = header[0].parse().unwrap();
        num_points = header[1].parse().unwrap();
        num_observations = header[2].parse().unwrap();

        let mut point_index = vec![0; num_observations as usize];
        let mut camera_index = vec![0; num_observations as usize];
        let mut observations = vec![0.0; 2 * num_observations as usize];

        let num_parameters = 9 * num_cameras + 3 * num_points;
        let mut parameters = vec![0.0; num_parameters as usize];

        // 读取观测数据
        for i in 0..num_observations {
            let mut line = String::new();
            reader.read_line(&mut line).expect("Unable to read observation");
            let obs: Vec<&str> = line.trim().split_whitespace().collect();
            camera_index[i as usize] = obs[0].parse().unwrap();
            point_index[i as usize] = obs[1].parse().unwrap();
            observations[2 * i as usize] = obs[2].parse().unwrap();
            observations[2 * i as usize + 1] = obs[3].parse().unwrap();
        }

        // 读取参数数据
        for i in 0..num_parameters {
            let mut line = String::new();
            reader.read_line(&mut line).expect("Unable to read parameter");
            parameters[i as usize] = line.trim().parse().unwrap();
        }

        if use_quaternions {
            // 将角轴转换为四元数
            let num_parameters = 10 * num_cameras + 3 * num_points;
            let mut quaternion_parameters = vec![0.0; num_parameters as usize];
            let mut original_cursor = 0;
            let mut quaternion_cursor = 0;

            for _ in 0..num_cameras {

                let angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [parameters[original_cursor], parameters[original_cursor + 1], parameters[original_cursor + 2]];
                let mut quaternion_arr: [f64; 4] = [0.0; 4];
                angle_axis_to_quaternion(&angle_axis_arr, &mut quaternion_arr);

                quaternion_cursor += 4;
                original_cursor += 3;
                for j in 4..10 {
                    quaternion_parameters[quaternion_cursor] = parameters[original_cursor];
                    quaternion_cursor += 1;
                    original_cursor += 1;
                }
            }

            // 复制剩余的点数据
            for i in 0..3 * num_points {
                quaternion_parameters[quaternion_cursor] = parameters[original_cursor];
                quaternion_cursor += 1;
                original_cursor += 1;
            }

            parameters = quaternion_parameters;
        }

        BALProblem {
            num_cameras,
            num_points,
            num_observations,
            num_parameters,
            use_quaternions,
            point_index,
            camera_index,
            observations,
            parameters,
        }
    }

    /// 将结果保存到文本文件
    pub fn write_to_file(&self, filename: &str) -> io::Result<()> {
        let mut file = BufWriter::new(File::create(filename)?);

        writeln!(file, "{} {} {} {}", self.num_cameras, self.num_cameras, self.num_points, self.num_observations)?;

        for i in 0..self.num_observations {
            write!(file, "{} {}", self.camera_index[i as usize], self.point_index[i as usize])?;
            for j in 0..2 {
                write!(file, " {}", self.observations[2 * i as usize + j])?;
            }
            writeln!(file)?;
        }

        for i in 0..self.num_cameras() {
            let mut angleaxis = [0.0; 9];
            if self.use_quaternions {
                let quaternion: [f64; 4] = [
                    self.parameters[10 * i as usize],
                    self.parameters[10 * i as usize + 1],
                    self.parameters[10 * i as usize + 2],
                    self.parameters[10 * i as usize + 3],
                ];
                let mut angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [0.0; 3];
                quaternion_to_angle_axis(&quaternion, &mut angle_axis_arr);
                angleaxis[0..3].copy_from_slice(&angle_axis_arr);
                angleaxis[3..9].copy_from_slice(&self.parameters[10 * i as usize + 4..10 * i as usize + 10]);
            } else {
                angleaxis[0..9].copy_from_slice(&self.parameters[9 * i as usize..9 * i as usize + 9]);
            }
            for j in 0..9 {
                writeln!(file, "{:.16}", angleaxis[j])?;
            }
        }

        let points = &self.parameters[self.camera_block_size() as usize * self.num_cameras as usize..];
        for i in 0..self.num_points() {
            let point = &points[i as usize * self.point_block_size() as usize..];
            for j in 0..self.point_block_size() {
                writeln!(file, "{:.16}", point[j as usize])?;
            }
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    /// 将问题保存为PLY文件以便在Meshlab或CloudCompare中检查
    pub fn write_to_ply_file(&self, filename: &str) -> io::Result<()> {
        let mut file = BufWriter::new(File::create(filename)?);

        writeln!(file, "ply")?;
        writeln!(file, "format ascii 1.0")?;
        writeln!(file, "element vertex {}", self.num_cameras + self.num_points)?;
        writeln!(file, "property float x")?;
        writeln!(file, "property float y")?;
        writeln!(file, "property float z")?;
        writeln!(file, "property uchar red")?;
        writeln!(file, "property uchar green")?;
        writeln!(file, "property uchar blue")?;
        writeln!(file, "end_header")?;

        // 导出外参数据（即相机中心）为绿色点
        let mut angle_axis = [0.0; 3];
        let mut center = [0.0; 3];
        for i in 0..self.num_cameras() {
            let camera = &self.parameters[self.camera_block_size() as usize * i as usize..];
            self.camera_to_angle_axis_and_center(camera, &mut angle_axis, &mut center);
            writeln!(file, "{} {} {} 0 255 0", center[0], center[1], center[2])?;
        }

        // 导出结构（即3D点）为白色点
        let points = &self.parameters[self.camera_block_size() as usize * self.num_cameras as usize..];
        for i in 0..self.num_points() {
            let point = &points[i as usize * self.point_block_size() as usize..];
            for j in 0..self.point_block_size() {
                write!(file, "{} ", point[j as usize])?;
            }
            writeln!(file, "255 255 255")?;
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    /// 将相机参数转换为角轴和中心
    fn camera_to_angle_axis_and_center(&self, camera: &[f64], angle_axis: &mut [f64], center: &mut [f64]) {
        if self.use_quaternions {
            quaternion_to_angle_axis(camera, angle_axis);
        } else {
            angle_axis.copy_from_slice(&camera[0..3]);
        }

        // c = -R't
        let inverse_rotation = DVector::from_column_slice(angle_axis).map(|x| -x);
        // 修正 angle_axis_rotate_point 的调用
        let angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [angle_axis[0], angle_axis[1], angle_axis[2]];
        let pt_arr: [f64; 3] = [
            camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 6],
            camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 5],
            camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 4],
        ];
        let mut result_arr: [f64; 3] = [0.0; 3];
        angle_axis_rotate_point(&angle_axis_arr, &pt_arr, &mut result_arr);
        center.copy_from_slice(&result_arr);
    }

    /// 将角轴和中心转换为相机参数
    fn angle_axis_and_center_to_camera(&self, angle_axis: &[f64], center: &[f64], camera: &mut [f64]) {
        if self.use_quaternions {
            angle_axis_to_quaternion(angle_axis, &mut camera[0..4]);
        } else {
            camera[0..3].copy_from_slice(angle_axis);
        }

        // t = -R * c
        angle_axis_rotate_point(angle_axis, center, &mut camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 6..]);
        camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 6..].iter_mut().for_each(|x| *x *= -1.0);
    }

    /// 归一化
    pub fn normalize(&mut self) {
        // 计算几何的边际中值
        let mut tmp = vec![0.0; self.num_points as usize];
        let mut median_point = Vector3::zeros();
        let points = self.mutable_points();

        for i in 0..3 {
            for j in 0..self.num_points {
                tmp[j as usize] = points[3 * j as usize + i];
            }
            median_point[i] = median(&tmp);
        }

        for i in 0..self.num_points {
            let point = DVector::from_column_slice(&points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3]);
            tmp[i as usize] = (point - median_point).norm();
        }

        let median_absolute_deviation = median(&tmp);

        // 缩放使得结果的绝对中位差为100
        let scale = 100.0 / median_absolute_deviation;

        // X = scale * (X - median)
        for i in 0..self.num_points {
            let point = DVector::from_column_slice(&points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3]);
            let scaled_point = scale * (point - median_point);
            points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3].copy_from_slice(scaled_point.as_slice());
        }

        let cameras = self.mutable_cameras();
        let mut angle_axis = [0.0; 3];
        let mut center = [0.0; 3];
        for i in 0..self.num_cameras {
            let camera = &mut cameras[self.camera_block_size() as usize * i as usize..];
            self.camera_to_angle_axis_and_center(camera, &mut angle_axis, &mut center);
            // center = scale * (center - median)
            let scaled_center = scale * (DVector::from_column_slice(&center) - median_point);
            center.copy_from_slice(scaled_center.as_slice());
            self.angle_axis_and_center_to_camera(&angle_axis, &center, camera);
        }
    }

    /// 扰动
    pub fn perturb(&mut self, rotation_sigma: f64, translation_sigma: f64, point_sigma: f64) {
        assert!(point_sigma >= 0.0);
        assert!(rotation_sigma >= 0.0);
        assert!(translation_sigma >= 0.0);

        // 修正 perturb 方法中的借用冲突
        let num_points = self.num_points;
        let camera_block_size = self.camera_block_size();
        let points = self.mutable_points();
        if point_sigma > 0.0 {
            for i in 0..num_points {
                perturb_point3(point_sigma, &mut points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3]);
            }
        }

        for i in 0..self.num_cameras {
            let camera = &mut self.parameters[camera_block_size as usize * i as usize..];
            let mut angle_axis = [0.0; 3];
            let mut center = [0.0; 3];
            self.camera_to_angle_axis_and_center(camera, &mut angle_axis, &mut center);

            if rotation_sigma > 0.0 {
                perturb_point3(rotation_sigma, &mut angle_axis);
            }
            self.angle_axis_and_center_to_camera(&angle_axis, &center, camera);

            if translation_sigma > 0.0 {
                perturb_point3(translation_sigma, &mut camera[camera_block_size as usize - 6..]);
            }
        }

    } // end fn perturb

    /// 获取相机块大小
    pub fn camera_block_size(&self) -> i32 {
        if self.use_quaternions { 10 } else { 9 }
    }

    /// 获取点块大小
    pub fn point_block_size(&self) -> i32 {
        3
    }

    /// 获取相机数量
    pub fn num_cameras(&self) -> i32 {
        self.num_cameras
    }

    /// 获取点数量
    pub fn num_points(&self) -> i32 {
        self.num_points
    }

    /// 获取观测数量
    pub fn num_observations(&self) -> i32 {
        self.num_observations
    }

    /// 获取参数数量
    pub fn num_parameters(&self) -> i32 {
        self.num_parameters
    }

    /// 获取点索引
    pub fn point_index(&self) -> &[i32] {
        &self.point_index
    }

    /// 获取相机索引
    pub fn camera_index(&self) -> &[i32] {
        &self.camera_index
    }

    /// 获取观测数据
    pub fn observations(&self) -> &[f64] {
        &self.observations
    }

    /// 获取参数数据
    pub fn parameters(&self) -> &[f64] {
        &self.parameters
    }

    /// 获取相机参数
    pub fn cameras(&self) -> &[f64] {
        &self.parameters
    }

    /// 获取点参数
    pub fn points(&self) -> &[f64] {
        &self.parameters[self.camera_block_size() as usize * self.num_cameras as usize..]
    }

    /// 获取可变的相机参数
    pub fn mutable_cameras(&mut self) -> &mut [f64] {
        &mut self.parameters
    }

    /// 获取可变的点参数
    pub fn mutable_points(&mut self) -> &mut [f64] {
        let start = self.camera_block_size() as usize * self.num_cameras as usize;
        &mut self.parameters[start..]
    }

    /// 获取可变的相机参数（针对特定观测）
    pub fn mutable_camera_for_observation(&mut self, i: usize) -> &mut [f64] {
        let camera_block_size = self.camera_block_size();
        let start = self.camera_index[i] as usize * camera_block_size as usize;
        &mut self.parameters[start..start + camera_block_size as usize]
    }

    /// 获取可变的点参数（针对特定观测）
    pub fn mutable_point_for_observation(&mut self, i: usize) -> &mut [f64] {
        let camera_block_size = self.camera_block_size();
        let point_block_size = self.point_block_size();
        let start = camera_block_size as usize * self.num_cameras as usize + self.point_index[i] as usize * point_block_size as usize;
        &mut self.parameters[start..start + point_block_size as usize]
    }

    /// 获取相机参数（针对特定观测）
    pub fn camera_for_observation(&self, i: usize) -> &[f64] {
        let start = self.camera_index[i] as usize * self.camera_block_size() as usize;
        &self.parameters[start..start + self.camera_block_size() as usize]
    }

    /// 获取点参数（针对特定观测）
    pub fn point_for_observation(&self, i: usize) -> &[f64] {
        let start = self.camera_block_size() as usize * self.num_cameras as usize + self.point_index[i] as usize * self.point_block_size() as usize;
        &self.parameters[start..start + self.point_block_size() as usize]
    }
}

/// 扰动3D点
fn perturb_point3(sigma: f64, point: &mut [f64]) {
    for i in 0..3 {
        point[i] += rand_normal() * sigma;
    }
}

/// 计算中位数
fn median(data: &[f64]) -> f64 {
    let mut data = data.to_vec();
    data.sort_by(|a, b| a.partial_cmp(b).unwrap());
    data[data.len() / 2]
}
```

## 回答
根据报错信息，问题主要出在函数调用时传递的参数类型不匹配。具体来说，`quaternion_to_angle_axis`、`angle_axis_to_quaternion` 和 `angle_axis_rotate_point` 这些函数期望的参数是固定长度的数组引用（如 `&[T; 3]` 或 `&[T; 4]`），但实际传递的是动态长度的切片（如 `&[f64]`）。以下是需要修改的地方：

### 1. `quaternion_to_angle_axis` 调用修正
```rust
// 修改前
quaternion_to_angle_axis(camera, angle_axis);

// 修改后
let quaternion: [f64; 4] = [camera[0], camera[1], camera[2], camera[3]];
let mut angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [0.0; 3];
quaternion_to_angle_axis(&quaternion, &mut angle_axis_arr);
angle_axis.copy_from_slice(&angle_axis_arr);
```

### 2. `angle_axis_to_quaternion` 调用修正
```rust
// 修改前
angle_axis_to_quaternion(angle_axis, &mut camera[0..4]);

// 修改后
let angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [angle_axis[0], angle_axis[1], angle_axis[2]];
let mut quaternion_arr: [f64; 4] = [0.0; 4];
angle_axis_to_quaternion(&angle_axis_arr, &mut quaternion_arr);
camera[0..4].copy_from_slice(&quaternion_arr);
```

### 3. `angle_axis_rotate_point` 调用修正
```rust
// 修改前
angle_axis_rotate_point(angle_axis, center, &mut camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 6..]);

// 修改后
let angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [angle_axis[0], angle_axis[1], angle_axis[2]];
let center_arr: [f64; 3] = [center[0], center[1], center[2]];
let mut result_arr: [f64; 3] = [0.0; 3];
angle_axis_rotate_point(&angle_axis_arr, &center_arr, &mut result_arr);
camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 6..self.camera_block_size() as usize - 3].copy_from_slice(&result_arr);
```

### 4. `angle_axis_rotate_point` 调用修正（在 `camera_to_angle_axis_and_center` 中）
```rust
// 修改前
angle_axis_rotate_point(&angle_axis_arr, &pt_arr, &mut result_arr);

// 修改后
let angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [angle_axis[0], angle_axis[1], angle_axis[2]];
let pt_arr: [f64; 3] = [pt_arr[0], pt_arr[1], pt_arr[2]];
let mut result_arr: [f64; 3] = [0.0; 3];
angle_axis_rotate_point(&angle_axis_arr, &pt_arr, &mut result_arr);
```

### 5. `angle_axis_rotate_point` 调用修正（在 `angle_axis_and_center_to_camera` 中）
```rust
// 修改前
angle_axis_rotate_point(angle_axis, center, &mut camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 6..]);

// 修改后
let angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [angle_axis[0], angle_axis[1], angle_axis[2]];
let center_arr: [f64; 3] = [center[0], center[1], center[2]];
let mut result_arr: [f64; 3] = [0.0; 3];
angle_axis_rotate_point(&angle_axis_arr, &center_arr, &mut result_arr);
camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 6..self.camera_block_size() as usize - 3].copy_from_slice(&result_arr);
```

这些修改确保了传递给函数的参数类型与函数签名中期望的类型一致。

# 问题
代码:
```rs
/* start BAL问题 */

/// 从文件读入BAL dataset
pub struct BALProblem {
    num_cameras: i32,
    num_points: i32,
    num_observations: i32,
    num_parameters: i32,
    use_quaternions: bool,

    point_index: Vec<i32>,
    camera_index: Vec<i32>,
    observations: Vec<f64>,
    parameters: Vec<f64>,
}

impl BALProblem {
    /// 从文本文件加载BAL数据
    pub fn new(filename: &str, use_quaternions: bool) -> Self {
        // 打开文件
        let mut file = File::open(filename).expect("Unable to open file");
        let mut reader = BufReader::new(file);

        let mut num_cameras = 0;
        let mut num_points = 0;
        let mut num_observations = 0;

        // 读取头信息
        let mut line = String::new();
        reader.read_line(&mut line).expect("Unable to read header");
        let header: Vec<&str> = line.trim().split_whitespace().collect();
        num_cameras = header[0].parse().unwrap();
        num_points = header[1].parse().unwrap();
        num_observations = header[2].parse().unwrap();

        let mut point_index = vec![0; num_observations as usize];
        let mut camera_index = vec![0; num_observations as usize];
        let mut observations = vec![0.0; 2 * num_observations as usize];

        let num_parameters = 9 * num_cameras + 3 * num_points;
        let mut parameters = vec![0.0; num_parameters as usize];

        // 读取观测数据
        for i in 0..num_observations {
            let mut line = String::new();
            reader.read_line(&mut line).expect("Unable to read observation");
            let obs: Vec<&str> = line.trim().split_whitespace().collect();
            camera_index[i as usize] = obs[0].parse().unwrap();
            point_index[i as usize] = obs[1].parse().unwrap();
            observations[2 * i as usize] = obs[2].parse().unwrap();
            observations[2 * i as usize + 1] = obs[3].parse().unwrap();
        }

        // 读取参数数据
        for i in 0..num_parameters {
            let mut line = String::new();
            reader.read_line(&mut line).expect("Unable to read parameter");
            parameters[i as usize] = line.trim().parse().unwrap();
        }

        if use_quaternions {
            // 将角轴转换为四元数
            let num_parameters = 10 * num_cameras + 3 * num_points;
            let mut quaternion_parameters = vec![0.0; num_parameters as usize];
            let mut original_cursor = 0;
            let mut quaternion_cursor = 0;

            for _ in 0..num_cameras {

                let angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [parameters[original_cursor], parameters[original_cursor + 1], parameters[original_cursor + 2]];
                let mut quaternion_arr: [f64; 4] = [0.0; 4];
                angle_axis_to_quaternion(&angle_axis_arr, &mut quaternion_arr);

                quaternion_cursor += 4;
                original_cursor += 3;
                for j in 4..10 {
                    quaternion_parameters[quaternion_cursor] = parameters[original_cursor];
                    quaternion_cursor += 1;
                    original_cursor += 1;
                }
            }

            // 复制剩余的点数据
            for i in 0..3 * num_points {
                quaternion_parameters[quaternion_cursor] = parameters[original_cursor];
                quaternion_cursor += 1;
                original_cursor += 1;
            }

            parameters = quaternion_parameters;
        }

        BALProblem {
            num_cameras,
            num_points,
            num_observations,
            num_parameters,
            use_quaternions,
            point_index,
            camera_index,
            observations,
            parameters,
        }
    }

    /// 将结果保存到文本文件
    pub fn write_to_file(&self, filename: &str) -> io::Result<()> {
        let mut file = BufWriter::new(File::create(filename)?);

        writeln!(file, "{} {} {} {}", self.num_cameras, self.num_cameras, self.num_points, self.num_observations)?;

        for i in 0..self.num_observations {
            write!(file, "{} {}", self.camera_index[i as usize], self.point_index[i as usize])?;
            for j in 0..2 {
                write!(file, " {}", self.observations[2 * i as usize + j])?;
            }
            writeln!(file)?;
        }

        for i in 0..self.num_cameras() {
            let mut angleaxis = [0.0; 9];
            if self.use_quaternions {
                let quaternion: [f64; 4] = [
                    self.parameters[10 * i as usize],
                    self.parameters[10 * i as usize + 1],
                    self.parameters[10 * i as usize + 2],
                    self.parameters[10 * i as usize + 3],
                ];
                let mut angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [0.0; 3];
                quaternion_to_angle_axis(&quaternion, &mut angle_axis_arr);
                angleaxis[0..3].copy_from_slice(&angle_axis_arr);
                angleaxis[3..9].copy_from_slice(&self.parameters[10 * i as usize + 4..10 * i as usize + 10]);
            } else {
                angleaxis[0..9].copy_from_slice(&self.parameters[9 * i as usize..9 * i as usize + 9]);
            }
            for j in 0..9 {
                writeln!(file, "{:.16}", angleaxis[j])?;
            }
        }

        let points = &self.parameters[self.camera_block_size() as usize * self.num_cameras as usize..];
        for i in 0..self.num_points() {
            let point = &points[i as usize * self.point_block_size() as usize..];
            for j in 0..self.point_block_size() {
                writeln!(file, "{:.16}", point[j as usize])?;
            }
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    /// 将问题保存为PLY文件以便在Meshlab或CloudCompare中检查
    pub fn write_to_ply_file(&self, filename: &str) -> io::Result<()> {
        let mut file = BufWriter::new(File::create(filename)?);

        writeln!(file, "ply")?;
        writeln!(file, "format ascii 1.0")?;
        writeln!(file, "element vertex {}", self.num_cameras + self.num_points)?;
        writeln!(file, "property float x")?;
        writeln!(file, "property float y")?;
        writeln!(file, "property float z")?;
        writeln!(file, "property uchar red")?;
        writeln!(file, "property uchar green")?;
        writeln!(file, "property uchar blue")?;
        writeln!(file, "end_header")?;

        // 导出外参数据（即相机中心）为绿色点
        let mut angle_axis = [0.0; 3];
        let mut center = [0.0; 3];
        for i in 0..self.num_cameras() {
            let camera = &self.parameters[self.camera_block_size() as usize * i as usize..];
            self.camera_to_angle_axis_and_center(camera, &mut angle_axis, &mut center);
            writeln!(file, "{} {} {} 0 255 0", center[0], center[1], center[2])?;
        }

        // 导出结构（即3D点）为白色点
        let points = &self.parameters[self.camera_block_size() as usize * self.num_cameras as usize..];
        for i in 0..self.num_points() {
            let point = &points[i as usize * self.point_block_size() as usize..];
            for j in 0..self.point_block_size() {
                write!(file, "{} ", point[j as usize])?;
            }
            writeln!(file, "255 255 255")?;
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    /// 将相机参数转换为角轴和中心
    fn camera_to_angle_axis_and_center(&self, camera: &[f64], angle_axis: &mut [f64], center: &mut [f64]) {
        if self.use_quaternions {
            let quaternion: [f64; 4] = [camera[0], camera[1], camera[2], camera[3]];
            let mut angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [0.0; 3];
            quaternion_to_angle_axis(&quaternion, &mut angle_axis_arr);
            angle_axis.copy_from_slice(&angle_axis_arr);
        } else {
            angle_axis.copy_from_slice(&camera[0..3]);
        }

        // c = -R't
        let inverse_rotation = DVector::from_column_slice(angle_axis).map(|x| -x);
        // 修正 angle_axis_rotate_point 的调用
        let angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [angle_axis[0], angle_axis[1], angle_axis[2]];
        let pt_arr: [f64; 3] = [
            camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 6],
            camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 5],
            camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 4],
        ];
        let mut result_arr: [f64; 3] = [0.0; 3];
        let angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [angle_axis[0], angle_axis[1], angle_axis[2]];
        let pt_arr: [f64; 3] = [pt_arr[0], pt_arr[1], pt_arr[2]];
        let mut result_arr: [f64; 3] = [0.0; 3];
        angle_axis_rotate_point(&angle_axis_arr, &pt_arr, &mut result_arr);
        center.copy_from_slice(&result_arr);
    }

    /// 将角轴和中心转换为相机参数
    fn angle_axis_and_center_to_camera(&self, angle_axis: &[f64], center: &[f64], camera: &mut [f64]) {
        if self.use_quaternions {
            let angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [angle_axis[0], angle_axis[1], angle_axis[2]];
            let mut quaternion_arr: [f64; 4] = [0.0; 4];
            angle_axis_to_quaternion(&angle_axis_arr, &mut quaternion_arr);
            camera[0..4].copy_from_slice(&quaternion_arr);
        } else {
            camera[0..3].copy_from_slice(angle_axis);
        }

        // t = -R * c
        let angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [angle_axis[0], angle_axis[1], angle_axis[2]];
        let center_arr: [f64; 3] = [center[0], center[1], center[2]];
        let mut result_arr: [f64; 3] = [0.0; 3];
        angle_axis_rotate_point(&angle_axis_arr, &center_arr, &mut result_arr);
        camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 6..self.camera_block_size() as usize - 3].copy_from_slice(&result_arr);
    }

    /// 归一化
    pub fn normalize(&mut self) {
        // 计算几何的边际中值
        let mut tmp = vec![0.0; self.num_points as usize];
        let mut median_point = Vector3::zeros();
        let points = self.mutable_points();

        for i in 0..3 {
            for j in 0..self.num_points {
                tmp[j as usize] = points[3 * j as usize + i];
            }
            median_point[i] = median(&tmp);
        }

        for i in 0..self.num_points {
            let point = DVector::from_column_slice(&points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3]);
            tmp[i as usize] = (point - median_point).norm();
        }

        let median_absolute_deviation = median(&tmp);

        // 缩放使得结果的绝对中位差为100
        let scale = 100.0 / median_absolute_deviation;

        // X = scale * (X - median)
        for i in 0..self.num_points {
            let point = DVector::from_column_slice(&points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3]);
            let scaled_point = scale * (point - median_point);
            points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3].copy_from_slice(scaled_point.as_slice());
        }

        let cameras = self.mutable_cameras();
        let mut angle_axis = [0.0; 3];
        let mut center = [0.0; 3];
        for i in 0..self.num_cameras {
            let camera = &mut cameras[self.camera_block_size() as usize * i as usize..];
            self.camera_to_angle_axis_and_center(camera, &mut angle_axis, &mut center);
            // center = scale * (center - median)
            let scaled_center = scale * (DVector::from_column_slice(&center) - median_point);
            center.copy_from_slice(scaled_center.as_slice());
            self.angle_axis_and_center_to_camera(&angle_axis, &center, camera);
        }
    }

    /// 扰动
    pub fn perturb(&mut self, rotation_sigma: f64, translation_sigma: f64, point_sigma: f64) {
        assert!(point_sigma >= 0.0);
        assert!(rotation_sigma >= 0.0);
        assert!(translation_sigma >= 0.0);

        // 修正 perturb 方法中的借用冲突
        let num_points = self.num_points;
        let camera_block_size = self.camera_block_size();
        let points = self.mutable_points();
        if point_sigma > 0.0 {
            for i in 0..num_points {
                perturb_point3(point_sigma, &mut points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3]);
            }
        }

        for i in 0..self.num_cameras {
            let camera = &mut self.parameters[camera_block_size as usize * i as usize..];
            let mut angle_axis = [0.0; 3];
            let mut center = [0.0; 3];
            self.camera_to_angle_axis_and_center(camera, &mut angle_axis, &mut center);

            if rotation_sigma > 0.0 {
                perturb_point3(rotation_sigma, &mut angle_axis);
            }
            self.angle_axis_and_center_to_camera(&angle_axis, &center, camera);

            if translation_sigma > 0.0 {
                perturb_point3(translation_sigma, &mut camera[camera_block_size as usize - 6..]);
            }
        }

    } // end fn perturb

    /// 获取相机块大小
    pub fn camera_block_size(&self) -> i32 {
        if self.use_quaternions { 10 } else { 9 }
    }

    /// 获取点块大小
    pub fn point_block_size(&self) -> i32 {
        3
    }

    /// 获取相机数量
    pub fn num_cameras(&self) -> i32 {
        self.num_cameras
    }

    /// 获取点数量
    pub fn num_points(&self) -> i32 {
        self.num_points
    }

    /// 获取观测数量
    pub fn num_observations(&self) -> i32 {
        self.num_observations
    }

    /// 获取参数数量
    pub fn num_parameters(&self) -> i32 {
        self.num_parameters
    }

    /// 获取点索引
    pub fn point_index(&self) -> &[i32] {
        &self.point_index
    }

    /// 获取相机索引
    pub fn camera_index(&self) -> &[i32] {
        &self.camera_index
    }

    /// 获取观测数据
    pub fn observations(&self) -> &[f64] {
        &self.observations
    }

    /// 获取参数数据
    pub fn parameters(&self) -> &[f64] {
        &self.parameters
    }

    /// 获取相机参数
    pub fn cameras(&self) -> &[f64] {
        &self.parameters
    }

    /// 获取点参数
    pub fn points(&self) -> &[f64] {
        &self.parameters[self.camera_block_size() as usize * self.num_cameras as usize..]
    }

    /// 获取可变的相机参数
    pub fn mutable_cameras(&mut self) -> &mut [f64] {
        &mut self.parameters
    }

    /// 获取可变的点参数
    pub fn mutable_points(&mut self) -> &mut [f64] {
        let start = self.camera_block_size() as usize * self.num_cameras as usize;
        &mut self.parameters[start..]
    }

    /// 获取可变的相机参数（针对特定观测）
    pub fn mutable_camera_for_observation(&mut self, i: usize) -> &mut [f64] {
        let camera_block_size = self.camera_block_size();
        let start = self.camera_index[i] as usize * camera_block_size as usize;
        &mut self.parameters[start..start + camera_block_size as usize]
    }

    /// 获取可变的点参数（针对特定观测）
    pub fn mutable_point_for_observation(&mut self, i: usize) -> &mut [f64] {
        let camera_block_size = self.camera_block_size();
        let point_block_size = self.point_block_size();
        let start = camera_block_size as usize * self.num_cameras as usize + self.point_index[i] as usize * point_block_size as usize;
        &mut self.parameters[start..start + point_block_size as usize]
    }

    /// 获取相机参数（针对特定观测）
    pub fn camera_for_observation(&self, i: usize) -> &[f64] {
        let start = self.camera_index[i] as usize * self.camera_block_size() as usize;
        &self.parameters[start..start + self.camera_block_size() as usize]
    }

    /// 获取点参数（针对特定观测）
    pub fn point_for_observation(&self, i: usize) -> &[f64] {
        let start = self.camera_block_size() as usize * self.num_cameras as usize + self.point_index[i] as usize * self.point_block_size() as usize;
        &self.parameters[start..start + self.point_block_size() as usize]
    }
}

/// 扰动3D点
fn perturb_point3(sigma: f64, point: &mut [f64]) {
    for i in 0..3 {
        point[i] += rand_normal() * sigma;
    }
}

/// 计算中位数
fn median(data: &[f64]) -> f64 {
    let mut data = data.to_vec();
    data.sort_by(|a, b| a.partial_cmp(b).unwrap());
    data[data.len() / 2]
}

/* end BAL问题 */
```

## 回答
根据报错信息，主要问题是在 `normalize` 和 `perturb` 方法中，`self` 被同时借用为可变和不可变引用，导致 Rust 的借用检查器报错。为了解决这个问题，我们需要在借用 `self` 的可变引用时，避免同时借用不可变引用。

以下是需要修改的地方：

### 1. `normalize` 方法
在 `normalize` 方法中，我们需要在借用 `self.mutable_points()` 和 `self.mutable_cameras()` 时，避免同时借用 `self.num_points` 和 `self.num_cameras`。可以通过提前获取这些值来解决。

```rust
pub fn normalize(&mut self) {
    // 提前获取 num_points 和 num_cameras
    let num_points = self.num_points;
    let num_cameras = self.num_cameras;

    // 计算几何的边际中值
    let mut tmp = vec![0.0; num_points as usize];
    let mut median_point = Vector3::zeros();
    let points = self.mutable_points();

    for i in 0..3 {
        for j in 0..num_points {
            tmp[j as usize] = points[3 * j as usize + i];
        }
        median_point[i] = median(&tmp);
    }

    for i in 0..num_points {
        let point = DVector::from_column_slice(&points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3]);
        tmp[i as usize] = (point - median_point).norm();
    }

    let median_absolute_deviation = median(&tmp);

    // 缩放使得结果的绝对中位差为100
    let scale = 100.0 / median_absolute_deviation;

    // X = scale * (X - median)
    for i in 0..num_points {
        let point = DVector::from_column_slice(&points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3]);
        let scaled_point = scale * (point - median_point);
        points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3].copy_from_slice(scaled_point.as_slice());
    }

    let cameras = self.mutable_cameras();
    let mut angle_axis = [0.0; 3];
    let mut center = [0.0; 3];
    for i in 0..num_cameras {
        let camera = &mut cameras[self.camera_block_size() as usize * i as usize..];
        self.camera_to_angle_axis_and_center(camera, &mut angle_axis, &mut center);
        // center = scale * (center - median)
        let scaled_center = scale * (DVector::from_column_slice(&center) - median_point);
        center.copy_from_slice(scaled_center.as_slice());
        self.angle_axis_and_center_to_camera(&angle_axis, &center, camera);
    }
}
```

### 2. `perturb` 方法
在 `perturb` 方法中，我们需要在借用 `self.parameters` 时，避免同时借用 `self.num_cameras` 和 `self.camera_block_size()`。可以通过提前获取这些值来解决。

```rust
pub fn perturb(&mut self, rotation_sigma: f64, translation_sigma: f64, point_sigma: f64) {
    assert!(point_sigma >= 0.0);
    assert!(rotation_sigma >= 0.0);
    assert!(translation_sigma >= 0.0);

    // 提前获取 num_points 和 camera_block_size
    let num_points = self.num_points;
    let camera_block_size = self.camera_block_size();

    let points = self.mutable_points();
    if point_sigma > 0.0 {
        for i in 0..num_points {
            perturb_point3(point_sigma, &mut points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3]);
        }
    }

    for i in 0..self.num_cameras {
        let camera = &mut self.parameters[camera_block_size as usize * i as usize..];
        let mut angle_axis = [0.0; 3];
        let mut center = [0.0; 3];
        self.camera_to_angle_axis_and_center(camera, &mut angle_axis, &mut center);

        if rotation_sigma > 0.0 {
            perturb_point3(rotation_sigma, &mut angle_axis);
        }
        self.angle_axis_and_center_to_camera(&angle_axis, &center, camera);

        if translation_sigma > 0.0 {
            perturb_point3(translation_sigma, &mut camera[camera_block_size as usize - 6..]);
        }
    }
}
```

### 总结
通过提前获取 `num_points`、`num_cameras` 和 `camera_block_size` 等值，避免了在借用可变引用时同时借用不可变引用，从而解决了 Rust 的借用检查器报错的问题。

直接复制一份self作为self2,防止引用和借用问题;

## 回答
在 Rust 中，直接复制一个结构体实例可能会导致所有权和借用问题，尤其是在结构体包含引用或内部可变性时。为了避免这些问题，你可以通过克隆整个结构体来创建一个新的实例。假设你的结构体实现了 `Clone` trait，你可以使用 `clone` 方法来创建一个新的实例。

以下是如何在你的代码中复制 `self` 为 `self2` 的示例：

```rust
pub fn normalize(&mut self) {
    // 复制 self 为 self2
    let mut self2 = self.clone();

    // 提前获取 num_points、num_cameras 和 camera_block_size
    let num_points = self2.num_points;
    let num_cameras = self2.num_cameras;
    let camera_block_size = self2.camera_block_size() as usize;

    // 计算几何的边际中值
    let mut tmp = vec![0.0; num_points as usize];
    let mut median_point = Vector3::zeros();
    let points = self2.mutable_points();

    for i in 0..3 {
        for j in 0..num_points {
            tmp[j as usize] = points[3 * j as usize + i];
        }
        median_point[i] = median(&tmp);
    }

    for i in 0..num_points {
        let point = DVector::from_column_slice(&points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3]);
        tmp[i as usize] = (point - median_point).norm();
    }

    let median_absolute_deviation = median(&tmp);

    // 缩放使得结果的绝对中位差为100
    let scale = 100.0 / median_absolute_deviation;

    // X = scale * (X - median)
    for i in 0..num_points {
        let point = DVector::from_column_slice(&points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3]);
        let scaled_point = scale * (point - median_point);
        points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3].copy_from_slice(scaled_point.as_slice());
    }

    let cameras = self2.mutable_cameras();

    let mut angle_axis = [0.0; 3];
    let mut center = [0.0; 3];
    for i in 0..num_cameras {
        let camera = &mut cameras[camera_block_size * (i as usize)..];
        self2.camera_to_angle_axis_and_center(camera, &mut angle_axis, &mut center);
        // center = scale * (center - median)
        let scaled_center = scale * (DVector::from_column_slice(&center) - median_point);
        center.copy_from_slice(scaled_center.as_slice());
        self2.angle_axis_and_center_to_camera(&angle_axis, &center, camera);
    }

    // 将 self2 的内容复制回 self
    *self = self2;
}
```

### 关键点：
1. **克隆结构体**：使用 `self.clone()` 创建一个新的实例 `self2`，这样可以避免直接操作 `self` 导致的借用问题。
2. **操作 `self2`**：在 `self2` 上进行所有的修改操作。
3. **将 `self2` 复制回 `self`**：在函数结束时，将 `self2` 的内容复制回 `self`，以确保修改生效。

### 注意事项：
- 确保你的结构体实现了 `Clone` trait。如果结构体中的某些字段不支持克隆，你可能需要手动实现 `Clone` trait。
- 这种方法可能会增加内存使用量，因为你需要复制整个结构体。如果结构体非常大，可能需要考虑其他优化方法。

通过这种方式，你可以避免 Rust 中的借用和所有权问题，同时确保代码的正确性。

```rs
            points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3].copy_from_slice(scaled_point.as_slice());
        }
        let cameras = self.mutable_cameras();

        let mut angle_axis = [0.0; 3];
        let mut center = [0.0; 3];
        for i in 0..num_cameras {
            let camera = &mut cameras[camera_block_size * (i as usize)..];
            self.camera_to_angle_axis_and_center(camera, &mut angle_axis, &mut center);
            // center = scale * (center - median)
            let scaled_center = scale * (DVector::from_column_slice(&center) - median_point);
            center.copy_from_slice(scaled_center.as_slice());
            self.angle_axis_and_center_to_camera(&angle_axis, &center, camera);
        }

    }

    /// 扰动
    pub fn perturb(&mut self, rotation_sigma: f64, translation_sigma: f64, point_sigma: f64) {
        assert!(point_sigma >= 0.0);
        assert!(rotation_sigma >= 0.0);
        assert!(translation_sigma >= 0.0);
    
        // 提前复制参数
        let num_points = self.num_points;
        let camera_block_size = self.camera_block_size() as usize;
        let points = self.mutable_points();
        let num_cameras = self.num_cameras();

        if point_sigma > 0.0 {
            for i in 0..num_points {
                perturb_point3(point_sigma, &mut points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3]);
            }
        }
    
        for i in 0..num_cameras {
            let camera = &mut self.parameters[camera_block_size * (i as usize)..];
            let mut angle_axis = [0.0; 3];
            let mut center = [0.0; 3];
            self.camera_to_angle_axis_and_center(camera, &mut angle_axis, &mut center);
    
            if rotation_sigma > 0.0 {
                perturb_point3(rotation_sigma, &mut angle_axis);
            }
            self.angle_axis_and_center_to_camera(&angle_axis, &center, camera);
    
            if translation_sigma > 0.0 {
                perturb_point3(translation_sigma, &mut camera[camera_block_size - 6..]);
            }
        }

    } // end fn perturb
```

self引用借用问题使用std::cell::RefCell解决,不要修改函数签名;

## 回答
在Rust中，`RefCell` 是一种在运行时进行借用检查的内部可变性机制。它允许你在不可变引用的情况下修改数据，但会在运行时检查借用规则。为了使用 `RefCell` 来解决你遇到的问题，我们需要将 `self` 中的某些字段包装在 `RefCell` 中，并在需要时通过 `borrow()` 或 `borrow_mut()` 来获取不可变或可变引用。

以下是如何使用 `RefCell` 来解决你的问题的示例：

1. 首先，将 `self` 中的 `cameras` 和 `parameters` 字段包装在 `RefCell` 中。

```rust
use std::cell::RefCell;

struct BundleAdjustment {
    num_points: u32,
    num_cameras: u32,
    cameras: RefCell<Vec<f64>>,
    parameters: RefCell<Vec<f64>>,
    // 其他字段...
}

impl BundleAdjustment {
    fn mutable_cameras(&self) -> std::cell::RefMut<'_, Vec<f64>> {
        self.cameras.borrow_mut()
    }

    fn mutable_parameters(&self) -> std::cell::RefMut<'_, Vec<f64>> {
        self.parameters.borrow_mut()
    }

    fn camera_to_angle_axis_and_center(&self, camera: &[f64], angle_axis: &mut [f64], center: &mut [f64]) {
        // 实现逻辑...
    }

    fn angle_axis_and_center_to_camera(&self, angle_axis: &[f64], center: &[f64], camera: &mut [f64]) {
        // 实现逻辑...
    }

    pub fn normalize(&mut self) {
        // 提前获取 num_points、num_cameras 和 camera_block_size
        let num_points = self.num_points;
        let num_cameras = self.num_cameras;
        let camera_block_size = self.camera_block_size() as usize;

        // 计算几何的边际中值
        let mut tmp = vec![0.0; num_points as usize];
        let mut median_point = Vector3::zeros();
        let points = self.mutable_points();

        for i in 0..3 {
            for j in 0..num_points {
                tmp[j as usize] = points[3 * j as usize + i];
            }
            median_point[i] = median(&tmp);
        }

        for i in 0..num_points {
            let point = DVector::from_column_slice(&points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3]);
            tmp[i as usize] = (point - median_point).norm();
        }

        let median_absolute_deviation = median(&tmp);

        // 缩放使得结果的绝对中位差为100
        let scale = 100.0 / median_absolute_deviation;

        // X = scale * (X - median)
        for i in 0..num_points {
            let point = DVector::from_column_slice(&points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3]);
            let scaled_point = scale * (point - median_point);
            points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3].copy_from_slice(scaled_point.as_slice());
        }

        let mut cameras = self.mutable_cameras();

        let mut angle_axis = [0.0; 3];
        let mut center = [0.0; 3];
        for i in 0..num_cameras {
            let camera = &mut cameras[camera_block_size * (i as usize)..];
            self.camera_to_angle_axis_and_center(camera, &mut angle_axis, &mut center);
            // center = scale * (center - median)
            let scaled_center = scale * (DVector::from_column_slice(&center) - median_point);
            center.copy_from_slice(scaled_center.as_slice());
            self.angle_axis_and_center_to_camera(&angle_axis, &center, camera);
        }
    }

    pub fn perturb(&mut self, rotation_sigma: f64, translation_sigma: f64, point_sigma: f64) {
        assert!(point_sigma >= 0.0);
        assert!(rotation_sigma >= 0.0);
        assert!(translation_sigma >= 0.0);

        // 提前复制参数
        let num_points = self.num_points;
        let camera_block_size = self.camera_block_size() as usize;
        let points = self.mutable_points();
        let num_cameras = self.num_cameras();

        if point_sigma > 0.0 {
            for i in 0..num_points {
                perturb_point3(point_sigma, &mut points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3]);
            }
        }

        let mut parameters = self.mutable_parameters();
        for i in 0..num_cameras {
            let camera = &mut parameters[camera_block_size * (i as usize)..];
            let mut angle_axis = [0.0; 3];
            let mut center = [0.0; 3];
            self.camera_to_angle_axis_and_center(camera, &mut angle_axis, &mut center);

            if rotation_sigma > 0.0 {
                perturb_point3(rotation_sigma, &mut angle_axis);
            }
            self.angle_axis_and_center_to_camera(&angle_axis, &center, camera);

            if translation_sigma > 0.0 {
                perturb_point3(translation_sigma, &mut camera[camera_block_size - 6..]);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

### 关键点：
1. **`RefCell` 的使用**：我们将 `cameras` 和 `parameters` 字段包装在 `RefCell` 中，这样可以在需要时通过 `borrow_mut()` 获取可变引用。
2. **借用检查**：`RefCell` 在运行时检查借用规则，确保不会同时存在多个可变引用或可变与不可变引用共存的情况。

### 注意事项：
- 使用 `RefCell` 会引入一定的运行时开销，因为它需要在运行时进行借用检查。
- 如果 `RefCell` 的借用规则被违反（例如，尝试在已有可变引用的情况下再次获取可变引用），程序会 panic。

通过这种方式，你可以在不修改函数签名的情况下解决借用冲突问题。

# 问题
```rs
/* start BAL问题 */

/// 从文件读入BAL dataset
pub struct BALProblem {
    num_cameras: i32,
    num_points: i32,
    num_observations: i32,
    num_parameters: i32,
    use_quaternions: bool,

    point_index: Vec<i32>,
    camera_index: Vec<i32>,
    observations: Vec<f64>,
    parameters: Vec<f64>,
}

impl BALProblem {
    /// 从文本文件加载BAL数据
    pub fn new(filename: &str, use_quaternions: bool) -> Self {
        // 打开文件
        let mut file = File::open(filename).expect("Unable to open file");
        let mut reader = BufReader::new(file);

        let mut num_cameras = 0;
        let mut num_points = 0;
        let mut num_observations = 0;

        // 读取头信息
        let mut line = String::new();
        reader.read_line(&mut line).expect("Unable to read header");
        let header: Vec<&str> = line.trim().split_whitespace().collect();
        num_cameras = header[0].parse().unwrap();
        num_points = header[1].parse().unwrap();
        num_observations = header[2].parse().unwrap();

        let mut point_index = vec![0; num_observations as usize];
        let mut camera_index = vec![0; num_observations as usize];
        let mut observations = vec![0.0; 2 * num_observations as usize];

        let num_parameters = 9 * num_cameras + 3 * num_points;
        let mut parameters = vec![0.0; num_parameters as usize];

        // 读取观测数据
        for i in 0..num_observations {
            let mut line = String::new();
            reader.read_line(&mut line).expect("Unable to read observation");
            let obs: Vec<&str> = line.trim().split_whitespace().collect();
            camera_index[i as usize] = obs[0].parse().unwrap();
            point_index[i as usize] = obs[1].parse().unwrap();
            observations[2 * i as usize] = obs[2].parse().unwrap();
            observations[2 * i as usize + 1] = obs[3].parse().unwrap();
        }

        // 读取参数数据
        for i in 0..num_parameters {
            let mut line = String::new();
            reader.read_line(&mut line).expect("Unable to read parameter");
            parameters[i as usize] = line.trim().parse().unwrap();
        }

        if use_quaternions {
            // 将角轴转换为四元数
            let num_parameters = 10 * num_cameras + 3 * num_points;
            let mut quaternion_parameters = vec![0.0; num_parameters as usize];
            let mut original_cursor = 0;
            let mut quaternion_cursor = 0;

            for _ in 0..num_cameras {

                let angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [parameters[original_cursor], parameters[original_cursor + 1], parameters[original_cursor + 2]];
                let mut quaternion_arr: [f64; 4] = [0.0; 4];
                angle_axis_to_quaternion(&angle_axis_arr, &mut quaternion_arr);

                quaternion_cursor += 4;
                original_cursor += 3;
                for j in 4..10 {
                    quaternion_parameters[quaternion_cursor] = parameters[original_cursor];
                    quaternion_cursor += 1;
                    original_cursor += 1;
                }
            }

            // 复制剩余的点数据
            for i in 0..3 * num_points {
                quaternion_parameters[quaternion_cursor] = parameters[original_cursor];
                quaternion_cursor += 1;
                original_cursor += 1;
            }

            parameters = quaternion_parameters;
        }

        BALProblem {
            num_cameras,
            num_points,
            num_observations,
            num_parameters,
            use_quaternions,
            point_index,
            camera_index,
            observations,
            parameters,
        }
    }

    /// 将结果保存到文本文件
    pub fn write_to_file(&self, filename: &str) -> io::Result<()> {
        let mut file = BufWriter::new(File::create(filename)?);

        writeln!(file, "{} {} {} {}", self.num_cameras, self.num_cameras, self.num_points, self.num_observations)?;

        for i in 0..self.num_observations {
            write!(file, "{} {}", self.camera_index[i as usize], self.point_index[i as usize])?;
            for j in 0..2 {
                write!(file, " {}", self.observations[2 * i as usize + j])?;
            }
            writeln!(file)?;
        }

        for i in 0..self.num_cameras() {
            let mut angleaxis = [0.0; 9];
            if self.use_quaternions {
                let quaternion: [f64; 4] = [
                    self.parameters[10 * i as usize],
                    self.parameters[10 * i as usize + 1],
                    self.parameters[10 * i as usize + 2],
                    self.parameters[10 * i as usize + 3],
                ];
                let mut angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [0.0; 3];
                quaternion_to_angle_axis(&quaternion, &mut angle_axis_arr);
                angleaxis[0..3].copy_from_slice(&angle_axis_arr);
                angleaxis[3..9].copy_from_slice(&self.parameters[10 * i as usize + 4..10 * i as usize + 10]);
            } else {
                angleaxis[0..9].copy_from_slice(&self.parameters[9 * i as usize..9 * i as usize + 9]);
            }
            for j in 0..9 {
                writeln!(file, "{:.16}", angleaxis[j])?;
            }
        }

        let points = &self.parameters[self.camera_block_size() as usize * self.num_cameras as usize..];
        for i in 0..self.num_points() {
            let point = &points[i as usize * self.point_block_size() as usize..];
            for j in 0..self.point_block_size() {
                writeln!(file, "{:.16}", point[j as usize])?;
            }
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    /// 将问题保存为PLY文件以便在Meshlab或CloudCompare中检查
    pub fn write_to_ply_file(&self, filename: &str) -> io::Result<()> {
        let mut file = BufWriter::new(File::create(filename)?);

        writeln!(file, "ply")?;
        writeln!(file, "format ascii 1.0")?;
        writeln!(file, "element vertex {}", self.num_cameras + self.num_points)?;
        writeln!(file, "property float x")?;
        writeln!(file, "property float y")?;
        writeln!(file, "property float z")?;
        writeln!(file, "property uchar red")?;
        writeln!(file, "property uchar green")?;
        writeln!(file, "property uchar blue")?;
        writeln!(file, "end_header")?;

        // 导出外参数据（即相机中心）为绿色点
        let mut angle_axis = [0.0; 3];
        let mut center = [0.0; 3];
        for i in 0..self.num_cameras() {
            let camera = &self.parameters[self.camera_block_size() as usize * i as usize..];
            self.camera_to_angle_axis_and_center(camera, &mut angle_axis, &mut center);
            writeln!(file, "{} {} {} 0 255 0", center[0], center[1], center[2])?;
        }

        // 导出结构（即3D点）为白色点
        let points = &self.parameters[self.camera_block_size() as usize * self.num_cameras as usize..];
        for i in 0..self.num_points() {
            let point = &points[i as usize * self.point_block_size() as usize..];
            for j in 0..self.point_block_size() {
                write!(file, "{} ", point[j as usize])?;
            }
            writeln!(file, "255 255 255")?;
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    /// 将相机参数转换为角轴和中心
    fn camera_to_angle_axis_and_center(&self, camera: &[f64], angle_axis: &mut [f64], center: &mut [f64]) {
        if self.use_quaternions {
            let quaternion: [f64; 4] = [camera[0], camera[1], camera[2], camera[3]];
            let mut angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [0.0; 3];
            quaternion_to_angle_axis(&quaternion, &mut angle_axis_arr);
            angle_axis.copy_from_slice(&angle_axis_arr);
        } else {
            angle_axis.copy_from_slice(&camera[0..3]);
        }

        // c = -R't
        let inverse_rotation = DVector::from_column_slice(angle_axis).map(|x| -x);
        // 修正 angle_axis_rotate_point 的调用
        let angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [angle_axis[0], angle_axis[1], angle_axis[2]];
        let pt_arr: [f64; 3] = [
            camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 6],
            camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 5],
            camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 4],
        ];
        let mut result_arr: [f64; 3] = [0.0; 3];
        let angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [angle_axis[0], angle_axis[1], angle_axis[2]];
        let pt_arr: [f64; 3] = [pt_arr[0], pt_arr[1], pt_arr[2]];
        let mut result_arr: [f64; 3] = [0.0; 3];
        angle_axis_rotate_point(&angle_axis_arr, &pt_arr, &mut result_arr);
        center.copy_from_slice(&result_arr);
    }

    /// 将角轴和中心转换为相机参数
    fn angle_axis_and_center_to_camera(&self, angle_axis: &[f64], center: &[f64], camera: &mut [f64]) {
        if self.use_quaternions {
            let angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [angle_axis[0], angle_axis[1], angle_axis[2]];
            let mut quaternion_arr: [f64; 4] = [0.0; 4];
            angle_axis_to_quaternion(&angle_axis_arr, &mut quaternion_arr);
            camera[0..4].copy_from_slice(&quaternion_arr);
        } else {
            camera[0..3].copy_from_slice(angle_axis);
        }

        // t = -R * c
        let angle_axis_arr: [f64; 3] = [angle_axis[0], angle_axis[1], angle_axis[2]];
        let center_arr: [f64; 3] = [center[0], center[1], center[2]];
        let mut result_arr: [f64; 3] = [0.0; 3];
        angle_axis_rotate_point(&angle_axis_arr, &center_arr, &mut result_arr);
        camera[self.camera_block_size() as usize - 6..self.camera_block_size() as usize - 3].copy_from_slice(&result_arr);
    }

    /// 归一化
    pub fn normalize(&mut self) {
        // 提前获取 num_points、num_cameras 和 camera_block_size
        let num_points = self.num_points;
        let num_cameras = self.num_cameras;
        let camera_block_size = self.camera_block_size() as usize;
    
        // 计算几何的边际中值
        let mut tmp = vec![0.0; num_points as usize];
        let mut median_point = Vector3::zeros();
        let points = self.mutable_points();
    
        for i in 0..3 {
            for j in 0..num_points {
                tmp[j as usize] = points[3 * j as usize + i];
            }
            median_point[i] = median(&tmp);
        }
    
        for i in 0..num_points {
            let point = DVector::from_column_slice(&points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3]);
            tmp[i as usize] = (point - median_point).norm();
        }
    
        let median_absolute_deviation = median(&tmp);
    
        // 缩放使得结果的绝对中位差为100
        let scale = 100.0 / median_absolute_deviation;
    
        // X = scale * (X - median)
        for i in 0..num_points {
            let point = DVector::from_column_slice(&points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3]);
            let scaled_point = scale * (point - median_point);
            points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3].copy_from_slice(scaled_point.as_slice());
        }
        let cameras = self.mutable_cameras();

        let mut angle_axis = [0.0; 3];
        let mut center = [0.0; 3];
        for i in 0..num_cameras {
            let camera = &mut cameras[camera_block_size * (i as usize)..];
            self.camera_to_angle_axis_and_center(camera, &mut angle_axis, &mut center);
            // center = scale * (center - median)
            let scaled_center = scale * (DVector::from_column_slice(&center) - median_point);
            center.copy_from_slice(scaled_center.as_slice());
            self.angle_axis_and_center_to_camera(&angle_axis, &center, camera);
        }

    }

    /// 扰动
    pub fn perturb(&mut self, rotation_sigma: f64, translation_sigma: f64, point_sigma: f64) {
        assert!(point_sigma >= 0.0);
        assert!(rotation_sigma >= 0.0);
        assert!(translation_sigma >= 0.0);
    
        // 提前复制参数
        let num_points = self.num_points;
        let camera_block_size = self.camera_block_size() as usize;
        let points = self.mutable_points();
        let num_cameras = self.num_cameras();

        if point_sigma > 0.0 {
            for i in 0..num_points {
                perturb_point3(point_sigma, &mut points[3 * i as usize..3 * i as usize + 3]);
            }
        }
    
        for i in 0..num_cameras {
            let camera = &mut self.parameters[camera_block_size * (i as usize)..];
            let mut angle_axis = [0.0; 3];
            let mut center = [0.0; 3];
            self.camera_to_angle_axis_and_center(camera, &mut angle_axis, &mut center);
    
            if rotation_sigma > 0.0 {
                perturb_point3(rotation_sigma, &mut angle_axis);
            }
            self.angle_axis_and_center_to_camera(&angle_axis, &center, camera);
    
            if translation_sigma > 0.0 {
                perturb_point3(translation_sigma, &mut camera[camera_block_size - 6..]);
            }
        }

    } // end fn perturb

    /// 获取相机块大小
    pub fn camera_block_size(&self) -> i32 {
        if self.use_quaternions { 10 } else { 9 }
    }

    /// 获取点块大小
    pub fn point_block_size(&self) -> i32 {
        3
    }

    /// 获取相机数量
    pub fn num_cameras(&self) -> i32 {
        self.num_cameras
    }

    /// 获取点数量
    pub fn num_points(&self) -> i32 {
        self.num_points
    }

    /// 获取观测数量
    pub fn num_observations(&self) -> i32 {
        self.num_observations
    }

    /// 获取参数数量
    pub fn num_parameters(&self) -> i32 {
        self.num_parameters
    }

    /// 获取点索引
    pub fn point_index(&self) -> &[i32] {
        &self.point_index
    }

    /// 获取相机索引
    pub fn camera_index(&self) -> &[i32] {
        &self.camera_index
    }

    /// 获取观测数据
    pub fn observations(&self) -> &[f64] {
        &self.observations
    }

    /// 获取参数数据
    pub fn parameters(&self) -> &[f64] {
        &self.parameters
    }

    /// 获取相机参数
    pub fn cameras(&self) -> &[f64] {
        &self.parameters
    }

    /// 获取点参数
    pub fn points(&self) -> &[f64] {
        &self.parameters[self.camera_block_size() as usize * self.num_cameras as usize..]
    }

    /// 获取可变的相机参数
    pub fn mutable_cameras(&mut self) -> &mut [f64] {
        &mut self.parameters
    }

    /// 获取可变的点参数
    pub fn mutable_points(&mut self) -> &mut [f64] {
        let start = self.camera_block_size() as usize * self.num_cameras as usize;
        &mut self.parameters[start..]
    }

    /// 获取可变的相机参数（针对特定观测）
    pub fn mutable_camera_for_observation(&mut self, i: usize) -> &mut [f64] {
        let camera_block_size = self.camera_block_size();
        let start = self.camera_index[i] as usize * camera_block_size as usize;
        &mut self.parameters[start..start + camera_block_size as usize]
    }

    /// 获取可变的点参数（针对特定观测）
    pub fn mutable_point_for_observation(&mut self, i: usize) -> &mut [f64] {
        let camera_block_size = self.camera_block_size();
        let point_block_size = self.point_block_size();
        let start = camera_block_size as usize * self.num_cameras as usize + self.point_index[i] as usize * point_block_size as usize;
        &mut self.parameters[start..start + point_block_size as usize]
    }

    /// 获取相机参数（针对特定观测）
    pub fn camera_for_observation(&self, i: usize) -> &[f64] {
        let start = self.camera_index[i] as usize * self.camera_block_size() as usize;
        &self.parameters[start..start + self.camera_block_size() as usize]
    }

    /// 获取点参数（针对特定观测）
    pub fn point_for_observation(&self, i: usize) -> &[f64] {
        let start = self.camera_block_size() as usize * self.num_cameras as usize + self.point_index[i] as usize * self.point_block_size() as usize;
        &self.parameters[start..start + self.point_block_size() as usize]
    }
}

/// 扰动3D点
fn perturb_point3(sigma: f64, point: &mut [f64]) {
    for i in 0..3 {
        point[i] += rand_normal() * sigma;
    }
}

/// 计算中位数
fn median(data: &[f64]) -> f64 {
    let mut data = data.to_vec();
    data.sort_by(|a, b| a.partial_cmp(b).unwrap());
    data[data.len() / 2]
}

/* end BAL问题 */
```

```sh
...

    |                         --------------- mutable borrow occurs here
```
